#!/bin/sh
# Storage brain update e329c62 -> 0356c0b, as one text file. On the VM, as root:
#
#     sh storage-brain-update-0356c0b.txt /path/to/storage-mcp-servers
#
# It checks this file is complete, applies the changed files (91), sets Claude Code to plain "opus" with the 1M context
# off where you have not set your own model / that setting, runs the installer (your ports, tokens, Market items, notes,
# skills, plugins and MCP servers are kept) and restarts the web UI instances. Safe to run twice.
set -eu
DST=${1:-}
[ -n "$DST" ] && [ -f "$DST/install-vm.sh" ] || { echo "usage: sh $0 /path/to/storage-mcp-servers  (the folder that holds install-vm.sh)"; exit 1; }
[ "$(id -u)" = 0 ] || { echo "run it as root"; exit 1; }
W=$(mktemp -d); trap 'rm -rf "$W"' EXIT
sed -n '/^__PAYLOAD_BELOW__$/,$p' "$0" | tail -n +2 | base64 -d > "$W/pack.tgz" 2>/dev/null || { echo "this file is damaged or cut short: copy it again"; exit 1; }
echo "03ab263f7bf8437472c84ef2c6fa669d0d500f55caf9a5d6d209466393a3fcd7  $W/pack.tgz" | sha256sum -c --quiet || { echo "this file is damaged or cut short: copy it again"; exit 1; }
tar -xzf "$W/pack.tgz" -C "$W"
sh "$W"/update-*/apply-update.sh "$DST"
[ "${PAAS_UPDATE_FILES_ONLY:-0}" = 1 ] && { echo "files applied (PAAS_UPDATE_FILES_ONLY=1: installer not run)"; exit 0; }
# plain opus, 1M context off: Claude Code otherwise assumes a 1M window for GLM and compacts too late
for f in /etc/claude-code/managed-settings.json /root/.claude/settings.json; do
  [ -f "$f" ] || continue
  python3 - "$f" <<'PYEOF'
import json, sys
p = sys.argv[1]; s = json.load(open(p)); env = s.setdefault("env", {})
changed = [k for k, v in (("CLAUDE_CODE_DISABLE_1M_CONTEXT", "1"),) if k not in env]
for k in changed: env[k] = "1"
if "model" not in s: s["model"] = "opus"; changed.append("model=opus")
json.dump(s, open(p, "w"), indent=2)
print(p + ": " + (", ".join(changed) if changed else "already set, kept"))
PYEOF
  break
done
cd "$DST"
if [ -f /etc/paas/multiuser.conf ]; then sh brain/multiuser/install-multiuser.sh; else sh install-vm.sh; fi
systemctl restart 'paas-ui@*' 2>/dev/null || true
echo "update done: $(head -n1 "$DST/UPDATE-LEVEL")"
exit 0
__PAYLOAD_BELOW__
